# items-revised

Configuration reconciliation and native persistence are separate from proof that every mechanical clause executes correctly. The automation column records the remaining limitation; it is not a passing test result.

The final column is an open, per-Item review question generated from the current authoring data and recorded source reference. It does not claim that the comparison or execution review has been completed.
